# 3D field: documented functions (`field_3d`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31 and extended in the rounds of 2026-11-04. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../field_3d.md`](../field_3d.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 1394 (BOOT.BIN 77, rel_blackjack 5, rel_connection 5, rel_conv_machine 1, rel_cutin_viewer 545, rel_dodge 2, rel_duel_draw 742, rel_field 14, rel_reversi 3).

| `0x08823f78` | `void * field_3d_gim_emit_texture(void *list, void *gim, int frame)` | Appends the texture set-up of a GIM file to a GE display list. Inside the first block range of gim + 0x10 it finds the palette block (type 5) and the image block (type 4) with field_3d_gim_find_block; the image plane offset comes from the image's offset table (+0x18) indexed by frame modulo the frame count +0x2e when the image type +0x2c is 3, else the first entry. Writes the texture mode (format +0x04, swizzle +0x06) and texture level 0 (width +0x08, height +0x0a, buffer width = width rounded up to the alignment pitch-align +0x0e x 8 / bpp +0x0c); the data come from the file when gim +0x08 is 0 or "PSP" (0x505350) or gim +0x0c differs from frame, else from the VRAM address in gim +0x08. With a palette it picks the palette plane the same way and writes the CLUT mode (palette format, mask 0xff) and the CLUT load with a block count of 1 (index4) or 0x10 (index8), doubled for a 32-bit palette (format 3). Returns the advanced list pointer. Side effects: the display list. Callers: 3 (field_3d_set_texture, field_3d_gim_set_texture_immediate, field_3d_gim_set_texture_frame_immediate); exported, module callers via import stubs: draw(ui_field_cursor_animate_texture, field_3d_cutin_set_layer_texture, ...), rel_field(world_draw_pickup_sprite, world_draw_character_sprite, ...), rel_shop(shop_draw_card_thumb), rel_deck(ui_draw_card_thumbnail). |

| `0x0882e950` | `void * field_3d_draw_pass(void *list, void *tms, void *matrices, uint pass, void *draw_state, void *hook)` | Draws one pass of a TGMS mesh. Optional frustum culling (hook +0x24 non-zero: field_3d_test_model_visible). Sets the texture filter (1, 1); for skinned meshes (tms +0x3c bones > 0) resets the bone cache 0x08924b18 to 0xffff, for rigid ones pushes and multiplies the root matrix tms +0x20. Per material (tms +0x0c, count +0x28, 0xa4 bytes): flag bit 0 set is drawn when pass & 3 is not 1, clear when pass & 3 is not 2 (pass 1 = opaque, 2 = translucent); skipped when flag bit 3 is set and bit (material +0xa0) of the group mask draw_state[4] is set (set bits hide groups); flag bit 2 substitutes the animated copy draw_state[2] + (+0x98) x 0xa4; flag bit 1 disables back-face culling. For up to four 0x20-byte layers at +0x18 (a clear layer bit 0 ends the list): the draw_state[0] callback may bind its own texture, else the layer texture (or its animated copy draw_state[3] + index x 0x28) via field_3d_set_texture; colour +0x0c; lighting when 0x08924c24 bit 0 and layer bit 1 (material colours +0x04..+0x10, specular power +0x14); alpha test (bit 2, func/ref/mask at +0x10..+0x12); depth func +0x13; blending (bit 3, op/src/dst +0x14..+0x16, fixed colours +0x18/+0x1c); depth write (bit 4); alpha write mask (bit 5); the layer callback +0x08 is called with 0 before and 1 after the batches; the draw_state[1] hook is called before (a negative result skips the batches) and after; then field_3d_draw_material_batches. Ends with depth func 7, depth mask 0, pixel mask 0 and the texture offset/scale reset. Returns the advanced list. Side effects: the display list, the GUM matrix stack, the bone cache. Callers: 1 (field_3d_draw_model); exported (8 import stubs without callers). |

With a TGMA (+0x34): advances its frame +0x38 by the step, wrapping by TGMA +0x1c, and calls field_3d_apply_tma with the copies +0x2c/+0x30. Returns nothing. Side effects: the instance frames, flags and matrices, the animated copies. ## `rel_field`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x090d388c` | `void field_3d_set_shadow_basis(float *light_direction)` | Builds the shadow projection basis used by field_3d_draw_shadow_volume from a light direction: z = normalize(light_direction); x = cross(up, z) with the constant up vector 0x090fc190; y = cross(z, x); stores normalize(y) at 0x09105ac0, z at 0x09105ad0, normalize(x) at 0x09105ae0 and the constant row 0x090fc1a0 at 0x09105af0 (a 4 x 4 matrix used with system_gum_mult_matrix). VFPU helper calls (vcross, vnormalize) read from the Ghidra pseudo-ops; lower confidence. Side effects: writes the matrix at 0x09105ac0. Callers: 1 (world_draw_object_list). |
| `0x090d39a0` | `void field_3d_begin_shadow_stencil(void *list)` | Opens the stencil-shadow pass of the free-roam field: clears the stencil buffer to 0 (system_ge_write_clear with flags 2), disables texturing (GE state 9), disables depth writes (depth mask 1), enables the stencil test (state 3) with function ALWAYS (1), ref 0, mask 0xff, enables face culling (state 5) and masks all colour writes (pixel mask 0xffffff). Side effects: GE commands in list. Callers: 1 (world_draw_object_list). |
| `0x090d3a1c` | `void * field_3d_draw_shadow_volume(float radius, void *list, float *position)` | Draws one shadow volume into the stencil buffer (z-pass method): builds the model matrix translate(position) x translate(const 0x090fc1e0) x shadow basis (0x09105ac0, field_3d_set_shadow_basis) x scale(const) x translate(const) x scale(const) x scale(radius, k, radius); draws the volume mesh (a 0x30-vertex strip at 0x090fc200 and a 0x48-vertex triangle list at 0x090fc5c0, vertex type 0x143) twice: with stencil operation INCR (4) on depth pass and front face 1, then DECR (5) with front face 0. Returns the advanced display list. Side effects: GE commands, the GUM matrix stack (pushed and popped). Callers: 1 (world_draw_object_list). |
| `0x090d3c58` | `void field_3d_resolve_shadow_stencil(void *list)` | Closes the stencil-shadow pass: restores texturing, front face, depth writes and colour writes, then with the stencil test enabled (function LESS (4), ref 0, i.e. stencil > 0), texturing and depth test disabled, draws a full-screen 480 x 272 sprite in colour 0x80000000 with alpha blending (half-transparent black over every shadowed pixel); then disables the stencil test, re-enables texturing and the depth test and sets the colour to opaque white. Side effects: GE commands in list. Callers: 1 (world_draw_object_list). |
| `0x090d4744` | `void field_3d_begin_field_scene(void)` | Starts the free-roam field scene on GE layer 0 (system_ge_select_layer(0)): field_3d_setup_field_render_state, field_3d_set_field_camera, world_apply_light_colour, commits the layer pointer, then resets the GUM model matrix (mode 2) to identity. Side effects: GE commands on layer 0, GUM matrices. Callers: 1 (world_field_draw). |
| `0x090d4794` | `void field_3d_apply_field_scene_state(void)` | Read from the disassembly: chains three set-up calls through the display-list pointer they return: field_3d_setup_field_render_state(), field_3d_set_field_camera(list), world_apply_light_colour(list). No return value used by the callers. Side effects: field render state, camera (orthographic field projection) and light colour written to the display list. Callers: 3 (world_draw_object_list, world_draw_map, world_sandwich_roulette_draw_golden_egg). |
| `0x090d4908` | `void * field_3d_setup_field_render_state(void *list)` | Writes the base render state of the free-roam field into list: screen offset (0x710, 0x778), viewport centre (0x800, 0x800) 480 x 272, depth range 0xffff..0, blend function add/src-alpha/one-minus-src-alpha with blending off, scissor 480 x 272 enabled, alpha function GREATER ref 0 with the alpha test off, depth function GEQUAL with the depth test on, front face 1 and culling on, texturing off, smooth shading, clip planes on, colour 0xff0000ff, fog 50..100 in colour 0x404040 with fog off; loads an orthographic projection (system_gum_ortho(-240, 240, -136, 136, -10000, 10000)) multiplied by the scale vector 0x090fcb80, identity view and model matrices, clear colour/depth/stencil 0 and linear texture filtering. The return value is the advanced display list (the decompiler shows void; field_3d_begin_field_scene passes the result on). Side effects: GE commands, GUM matrices. Callers: 2 (field_3d_apply_field_scene_state, field_3d_begin_field_scene). |
| `0x090d4b24` | `void * field_3d_set_field_camera(void *list)` | Sets the free-roam field view matrix: target = the player position (world_get_player_position), eye = target + the offset 0x090f3e30/34/38, up = the constant 0x090fcb90; while the shake flag 0x09106710 is set, adds the shake vector 0x09106730 to eye and target; calls field_3d_camera_set_look_at, marks the matrices dirty, copies the camera's position (+0x30 of field_3d_get_camera) to 0x09106740 and resets the model matrix. Returns list unchanged. Side effects: GUM view and model matrices, 0x09106740. Callers: 2 (field_3d_apply_field_scene_state, field_3d_begin_field_scene). |
| `0x090da484` | `void field_3d_set_map_variant_group_mask(void *inst, int variant)` | Read from the disassembly (the decompiler loses the stores): builds a 32-bit hide mask in which bit i is set unless group i belongs to the variant: variant 1 keeps groups 5-9 visible, variant 2 groups 10-14, any other value groups 0-4 (all other bits set); then tail-jumps to field_3d_set_group_mask(inst, mask) through its import stub. So each map mesh carries up to three 5-group variants and the light period (world_get_map_variant_for_period) picks one. No return value. Side effects: writes the model instance's group mask (+0x64). Callers: 1 (world_bind_map_archive, once per map mesh). |
| `0x090db30c` | `int field_3d_map_stencil_draw_callback(void **list_ref, void *model, int mesh, int material, int phase)` | Per-material draw callback of the free-roam map that turns a mesh into a stencil writer (phase is the fifth argument, in register t0): phase 0 (before drawing) masks colour writes (pixel mask 0xffffff) and depth writes, enables the depth and stencil tests with function ALWAYS ref 1 and operation REPLACE on depth pass, so the mesh writes stencil 1 where it is visible; phase 1 (after) restores colour and depth writes. Writes the advanced display list back to *list_ref. model, mesh and material are not used. Returns 0. Side effects: GE commands. No direct caller (reached through a function pointer: the map model's draw callback). |
| `0x090db6f0` | `int field_3d_map_material_dd_callback(void **list_ref, void *model, int mesh, int material, int phase)` | Per-material draw callback of the free-roam map: reads the material name pointer at *(model +0xc) + mesh x 0xa4 + material x 0x20 + 0x1c and flags the material when the name is longer than 6 characters and contains one of four substrings (0x090fd16c, 0x090fd174, 0x090fd17c, 0x090fd184). For a flagged material it enables a GE state before drawing (phase 1) and disables it after (phase 0); which state is hidden by the decompiler (open question). Writes the list back to *list_ref. Returns 0. Side effects: GE commands. No direct caller (reached through a function pointer: the map model's draw callback). |
| `0x090db920` | `void field_3d_begin_map_stencil_pass(void *list)` | Opens the stencil pass of the free-roam map (same commands as field_3d_begin_shadow_stencil): clears the stencil to 0, disables texturing, disables depth writes, enables the stencil test with function ALWAYS ref 0 mask 0xff, enables culling and masks colour writes. Side effects: GE commands in list. Callers: 1 (world_draw_map). |
| `0x090db99c` | `void * field_3d_draw_map_stencil_volume(void *list, float *position)` | Draws a stencil volume at position for the free-roam map (z-pass inc/dec like field_3d_draw_shadow_volume): matrix translate(position) x translate(const 0x090fd1c0) x (rotate Y by a constant on map 3) x a Y/Z-flip matrix x scale(0x090fd1b0) x translate(0x090fd190) x scale(0x090fd1a0); on map 3 the scale is (1.75, k, const 0x090f80c0); on maps 0xd-0x14 and 0x26, when position x / 0.5 is 12 and z / 0.5 is 5, the z scale is reduced by 0x090f80c4 (world_get_current_map). Draws the mesh (a 15-vertex strip at 0x090fd1d0 and 6 triangles' vertices at 0x090fd248, vertex type 0xa1) with stencil operation INCR and front face 1, then DECR and front face 0. Returns the advanced list. Side effects: GE commands, the GUM matrix stack (pushed and popped). Callers: 1 (world_draw_map). |
| `0x090dbcbc` | `void field_3d_draw_stencil_pulse_overlay(void *list)` | Closes the map stencil pass with a pulsing tint: pulse = \|frame % 40 - 20\| / 20 from the frame counter (word 0 of system_get_frame_timing); restores texturing, front face, depth and colour writes, then with the stencil test LESS ref 0 (stencil > 0), texturing and depth test off, draws a full-screen 480 x 272 sprite in colour 0x8080ff with alpha 32 + 32 x pulse and alpha blending; finally disables the stencil test, re-enables texturing and the depth test and sets the colour to opaque white. Side effects: GE commands in list. Callers: 1 (world_draw_map). |

## `rel_dodge`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x00004244` | `void field_3d_eval_hermite_vec3(float t, float *out, float *p0, float *m0, float *p1, float *m1)` | Same code as the rel_duel_draw function: cubic Hermite interpolation of 3-vectors, out = (1-t)^2 (1+2t) p0 + t (1-t)^2 m0 - t^2 (1-t) m1 + t^2 (3-2t) p1, built from system_vfpu_vector3_scale and vector adds (arguments 5-6 in t0/t1). VFPU code read from the Ghidra pseudo-ops; lower confidence. No return value. Side effects: writes out. Callers: 1 (world_dodge_player_update, the jump arc). |
| `0x00004360` | `float *field_3d_copy_vec3(float *dest, float *src)` | Same code as the BOOT.BIN function: copies three floats from src to dest and returns dest. Side effects: writes dest[0..2]. Callers: 1 (field_3d_eval_hermite_vec3). |
